Strengthen the two exported SKILL.md instructions so text-led 480x480 reminders prioritize large, safe-area title/time content and animate that primary information before adding peripheral decoration, as localized by the permitted analyzer evidence.

replace_markdown_blockSKILL.md

The permitted analyzer evidence reports that SYNC and 10:30 were too small while upper-canvas whitespace and peripheral decoration consumed space; this tightens only the exported message-size instruction.

预期：Improves task correctness, output quality, and skill gain for text-led message GIFs by making the title and time visibly salient on the 480x480 canvas. · 风险：low
replace_markdown_blockSKILL.md

The permitted analyzer evidence attributes the low blind score to motion concentrated on outer dots and arcs rather than the title or time; the replacement preserves the existing creativity instruction and adds a bounded priority rule.

预期：Directs animation toward the reminder's primary information while retaining the existing creative-combination behavior for other GIF concepts. · 风险：low

查看 Patch 操作与技术引用

The supplied train evidence records a -0.116075 paired skill-gain delta for the emoji-bounce task and localizes a possible scope leak from two status-label instructions. Gate both instructions to fixed-region staged-status tasks and explicitly preserve emoji motion.

replace_markdown_blockSKILL.md

The evidence reports the emoji-bounce regression and identifies these status-only motion constraints as a possible scope leak rather than proven causality. A strict trigger plus explicit emoji exemption directly tests that localization while preserving task-specified status behavior.

预期：Prevent status-label position and motion constraints from suppressing emoji bounce behavior, improving task_correctness, output_quality, and skill_gain on the affected train task. · 风险：low
replace_markdown_blockSKILL.md

The supplied recommendation calls for strict triggers on both status-text instructions and shorter wording. This bounded replacement limits formatting requirements to fixed-region staged labels and explicitly excludes animated subjects.

预期：Keep oracle-friendly status text guidance available when requested without imposing irrelevant constraints on emoji-led animation, supporting recovery of task_correctness, output_quality, and skill_gain. · 风险：low

Train evidence links the emoji-bounce regression in task_correctness, output_quality, and skill_gain to a text-led hierarchy rule with overly broad scope; this proposal narrows that rule while preserving general animation-combination guidance.

replace_markdown_blockSKILL.md

Explicit task-type scoping removes that interference while retaining the reminder-specific readability rule.

预期：Prevent text-led title/time priorities from constraining emoji bounce motion, aiming to restore task_correctness toward 1.0 and recover output_quality and skill_gain without changing package reliability. · 风险：low

04 / QUALITY × COST

质量提升与相对资源成本

横轴是 Candidate 相对 frozen original 的成本比，纵轴是 held-out validation Δ。1.0× 是 original 基准，policy 中的极端成本线为 2.0×。六维条形由 Python 投影预先计算，浏览器不决定排序或 Gate。

更省资源 ← relative cost → 更高成本validation Δ 越高越好 ↑

05 / TASK-NATIVE OUTPUTS

按任务对照真实 GIF

默认展示 held-out validation 与排名第一的 Candidate。每组将 no-skill、original 和所选 Candidate 放在同一任务下；证据不完整的候选会保留状态标记，不会混成 accepted 结果。

验证任务 01functional-validation-efficiency-0075 份真实产物

为 Slack 消息制作一个紧凑的庆祝 GIF：480×480 画布中央是清楚的勾号，周围粒子向外爆发后渐隐。请在动作顺滑和轮廓可读的前提下控制帧率、调色板与文件体积，严格遵循随附的体积和帧率预算，并输出指定文件。

不使用 Skill

Reference
assertions 5/6独立评分 0.961时长 60.0s工具 4

成品在很充裕的体积余量内同时保住了清晰主体和完整粒子爆发过程，压缩与视觉质量平衡优秀。若继续精修，可让最早几帧的勾号成形过渡更均匀，但当前结果已充分满足紧凑、清楚和顺滑的要求。

08 / EVIDENCE & REPRODUCTION

证据与复现

Policy、frontier ranking、process 与 Runtime 原始投影
